### [69] [Distributed Generative Inference of LLM at Internet Scales with Multi-Dimensional Communication Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21072)
*Jiu Chen,Shuangyan Yang,Xu Xiong,Hexiao Duan,Xinran Zhang,Jie Ren,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出BloomBee，一个互联网规模的分布式LLM推理框架，通过优化通信带宽瓶颈，显著提升了服务吞吐量并降低了延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化LLM推理利用互联网上的异构节点提供了一种高性能且低成本的替代方案，但节点间网络带宽较低导致通信成为主要瓶颈，限制了系统性能。

Method: 提出BloomBee框架，整合LLM层分配、微批次处理和张量卸载技术，利用动态规划求解优化问题；同时针对低带宽网络定制了无损压缩和推测解码技术以降低通信开销。

Result: 实验表明，与最先进的去中心化LLM推理系统相比，BloomBee将服务吞吐量提高了高达1.76倍，平均延迟降低了高达43.20%。

Conclusion: BloomBee有效解决了去中心化LLM推理中的通信瓶颈问题，在多种网络环境下均表现出优越的性能，并已开源。

Abstract: Decentralized LLM inference distributes computation among heterogeneous nodes across the internet, offering a performant and cost-efficient solution, alternative to traditional centralized inference. However, the low cross-node network bandwidth makes communication the primary bottleneck. In this paper, we introduce BloomBee, an internet-scale distributed LLM inference framework. BloomBee integrates LLM-layer assignment, micro-batching and tensor offloading to optimize communication from multiple dimensions. Additionally, BloomBee formulates the coordination of these techniques as an optimization problem and solves it using dynamic programming. BloomBee also customizes lossless compression and speculative decoding according to low-bandwidth network settings to reduce communication overhead. We evaluate BloomBee across a spectrum of network environments and show that it improves service throughput by up to 1.76x. It also reduces average latency by up to 43.20% compared to state-of-the-art decentralized LLM inference systems. BloomBee is open-sourced.

</details>


### [70] [TorchGWAS : GPU-accelerated GWAS for thousands of quantitative phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21095)
*Xingzhong Zhao,Ziqian Xie,Islam,Sheikh Muhammad Saiful,Tian Xia,Chen,Cheng,Degui Zhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TorchGWAS是一个基于GPU硬件加速的高通量全基因组关联分析（GWAS）框架，专为处理大规模表型数据而设计。相比传统CPU工具，在单块NVIDIA A100 GPU上实现了300-1700倍的表型分析吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生物信息学流程（特别是成像和表示学习）能从单个队列中生成成千上万的定量表型。在这种背景下，逐个性状运行全基因组关联分析会迅速成为计算瓶颈。现有的GWAS工具虽然对单个性状非常有效，但并未针对需要跨大表型面板重复使用相同基因型矩阵的表型富集筛选工作流进行优化。

Method: 提出了TorchGWAS框架，通过硬件加速实现大表型面板的高通量关联分析。该框架提供稳定的Python和命令行工作流，支持线性GWAS和多变量表型筛选，兼容NumPy、PLINK和BGEN基因型输入，能按样本标识符对齐表型和协变量表，并在内部执行协变量调整。

Result: 在包含890万个标记和23,000个样本的基准测试中，fastGWA在64核AMD EPYC CPU上每个表型约需100秒，而TorchGWAS在单块NVIDIA A100 GPU上完成2,048个表型仅需10分钟，完成20,480个表型仅需20分钟，相当于表型吞吐量提升约300至1700倍。

Conclusion: TorchGWAS使得在需要高效评估数千个定量性状的表型富集场景下，大规模GWAS筛选变得切实可行，显著解决了多表型分析的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Motivation: Modern bioinformatics workflows, particularly in imaging and representation learning, can generate thousands to tens of thousands of quantitative phenotypes from a single cohort. In such settings, running genome-wide association analyses trait by trait rapidly becomes a computational bottleneck. While established GWAS tools are highly effective for individual traits, they are not optimized for phenotype-rich screening workflows in which the same genotype matrix is reused across a large phenotype panel. Results: We present TorchGWAS, a framework for high-throughput association testing of large phenotype panels through hardware acceleration. The current public release provides stable Python and command-line workflows for linear GWAS and multivariate phenotype screening, supports NumPy, PLINK, and BGEN genotype inputs, aligns phenotype and covariate tables by sample identifier, and performs covariate adjustment internally. In a benchmark with 8.9 million markers and 23,000 samples, fastGWA required approximately 100 second per phenotype on an AMD EPYC 7763 64-core CPU, whereas TorchGWAS completed 2,048 phenotypes in 10 minute and 20,480 phenotypes in 20 minutes on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, corresponding to an approximately 300- to 1700-fold increase in phenotype throughput. TorchGWAS therefore makes large-scale GWAS screening practical in phenotype-rich settings where thousands of quantitative traits must be evaluated efficiently. Availability and implementation: TorchGWAS is implemented in Python and distributed as a documented source repository at https://github.com/ZhiGroup/TorchGWAS. The current release provides a command-line interface, packaged source code, tutorials, benchmark scripts, and example workflows.

</details>


### [71] [A Task Decomposition and Planning Framework for Efficient LLM Inference in AI-Enabled WiFi-Offload Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21399)
*Mingqi Han,Xinghua Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用户-边缘协作框架，通过LLM规划器进行任务分解和难度预测，并设计了分解感知调度策略，在WiFi网络中实现了高效的LLM推理卸载，相比基线方案延迟降低20%、奖励提升80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI WiFi卸载为资源受限的无线设备提供大语言模型服务提供了新途径，但与传统的边缘计算不同，WiFi上的LLM推理必须同时解决异构模型能力、无线竞争、任务复杂性不确定性以及推理任务之间的语义相关性等问题。现有方法难以在多用户多边缘WiFi网络中实现高效的LLM推理卸载。

Method: 提出一个用户-边缘协作框架，包含基于LLM的规划器，该规划器不仅执行任务分解，还推断子任务难度和预期输出token长度，从而更准确地估计异构节点上的执行质量和延迟。基于这些估计，设计了分解感知调度策略，在通信、排队和计算约束下联合优化子任务分配、执行和聚合。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的框架相比仅本地执行和最近边缘基线实现了更好的延迟-准确性权衡，平均延迟降低20%，整体奖励提高80%。此外，蒸馏的轻量级规划器接近大型教师模型的性能，同时更适合实际的边缘部署。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了WiFi网络中LLM推理卸载的挑战，证明了用户-边缘协作框架在处理异构模型能力、无线竞争和任务复杂性方面的有效性，为资源受限设备提供了高效的LLM服务解决方案，轻量级规划器的蒸馏实现使其具备了实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: AI WiFi offload is emerging as a promising approach for providing large language model (LLM) services to resource-constrained wireless devices. However, unlike conventional edge computing, LLM inference over WiFi must jointly address heterogeneous model capabilities, wireless contention, uncertain task complexity, and semantic correlation among reasoning tasks. In this paper, we investigate LLM inference offloading in a multi-user multi-edge WiFi network, where each task can be executed locally, directly offloaded to a nearby edge access point (AP), or decomposed into multiple subtasks for collaborative execution across local and edge nodes. To this end, we propose a user-edge collaborative framework with an LLM-based planner that not only performs task decomposition but also infers subtask difficulty and expected output token length, enabling more accurate estimation of execution quality and latency on heterogeneous nodes. Based on these estimates, we further design a decomposition-aware scheduling strategy that jointly optimizes subtask assignment, execution, and aggregation under communication, queuing, and computation constraints. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves a better latency-accuracy tradeoff than local-only and nearest-edge baselines, reducing the average latency by $20\%$ and improving the overall reward by $80\%$. Moreover, the distilled lightweight planner approaches the performance of the large teacher model while remaining more suitable for practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [72] [A Cloud-Native Architecture for Human-in-Control LLM-Assisted OpenSearch in Investigative Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21125)
*Benjamin Puhani,Kai Brehmer,Malte Prieß*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向私有云的云原生微服务架构，通过集成大语言模型（LLM）将自然语言查询转化为OpenSearch DSL表达式，结合BM25与语义向量嵌入的混合检索策略，旨在解决复杂刑事调查中证据检索的语义鸿沟问题，并通过原型系统验证了技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂刑事调查常因大量非结构化证据与自然语言调查意图同技术检索逻辑间的语义鸿沟而受阻，亟需一种既能满足高安全与可扩展性要求，又能有效缩小该语义鸿沟的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种适用于私有云部署的云原生微服务架构；集成大语言模型（LLM）于“人在环中”（Human-in-Control）工作流，实现自然语言查询到有效OpenSearch DSL表达式的转换；在OpenSearch中实现结合BM25词汇检索与嵌套语义向量嵌入的混合检索策略。

Result: 构建并实现了一个功能性原型系统，成功验证了技术可行性，确立了系统架构基线；提出了使用Enron电子邮件数据集作为受限调查语料库结构代理的严谨评估方法论。

Conclusion: 该系统设计与初步功能验证表明所提架构具备可行性，为未来的实证评估奠定了坚实基础，有望有效提升复杂刑事调查中的证据检索效率与准确性。

Abstract: Complex criminal investigations are often hindered by large volumes of unstructured evidence and by the semantic gap between natural language investigative intent and technical search logic. To address this challenge, we present a design and feasibility study of a cloud-native microservice architecture tailored to private-cloud deployments, contributing to research in secure cloud computing and leveraging modern cloud paradigms under high security and scalability requirements. The proposed system integrates Large Language Models into a "Human-in-Control" workflow that translates natural-language queries into syntactically valid OpenSearch Domain-Specific Language expressions. We describe the implementation of a hybrid retrieval strategy within OpenSearch that combines BM25-based lexical search with nested semantic vector embeddings. The paper focuses on system design and preliminary functional validation, establishing an architectural baseline for future empirical evaluation. Technical feasibility is demonstrated through a functional prototype, and a rigorous evaluation methodology is outlined using the Enron Email Dataset as a structural proxy for restricted investigative corpora.

</details>


### [73] [Risk-Aware and Stable Edge Server Selection Under Network Latency SLOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21483)
*Mohan Liyanage,Arnova Abdullah,Eldiyar Zhantileuov,Rolf Schuster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向时延敏感应用的轻量级、可解释边缘服务器动态选择框架，通过显式评估尾部风险和引入切换滞后机制，在降低截止时间错过率的同时大幅减少了服务器切换频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态边缘计算环境中，传统的仅依赖均值的服务器选择方法容易忽视尾部风险，可能导致服务等级目标(SLO)违规；同时，网络波动容易引发服务器频繁切换（振荡），影响系统稳定性。因此，需要一种既能显式量化违规风险又能保证切换稳定性的决策框架。

Method: 该方法利用网络时延的预测均值和不确定性特征来刻画候选服务器。风险量化采用紧正态近似结合保守的Cantelli界限来评估SLO违规风险；决策层面引入基于百分位的评分机制和滞后机制，以抑制短时网络波动导致的振荡性切换。

Result: 在多服务器边缘测试平台（SLO阈值τ=0.5s）上的实验表明，相比仅考虑均值的基线方法，该方法将截止时间错过率从39%降至34%，服务器切换频率从46%降至5.5%（降低约88%），同时保持平均时延约为0.45s（满足SLO要求）。

Conclusion: 显式的风险评估与稳定性保持控制相结合，能够实现动态边缘环境下实用且鲁棒的自适应服务器选择，有效平衡了服务性能与系统稳定性。

Abstract: We present a lightweight and interpretable decision framework for dynamic edge server selection in latency-critical applications that explicitly accounts for tail risk and switching stability. Each candidate server is characterised by predictive mean and uncertainty summaries of network latency, which are used to estimate the risk of service-level objective (SLO) violations and to guide selection. Risk is evaluated using a tight Normal approximation complemented by a conservative Cantelli bound, while percentile-based scoring coupled with hysteresis stabilizes decisions and suppresses oscillatory switching under short-lived network fluctuations.
  Experimental results on a multi-server edge testbed with a strict SLO of $τ= 0.5$\,s show that the proposed approach reduces the deadline-miss rate from 39\% to 34\% compared to a mean-only baseline, while reducing switching frequency from 46\% to 5.5\% ($\approx$88\% reduction) and maintaining sub-SLO average latency ($\approx$0.45\,s). These results demonstrate that explicit risk evaluation combined with stability-preserving control enables practical and robust adaptive server selection in dynamic edge environments.

</details>


### [74] [Optimizing High-Throughput Distributed Data Pipelines for Reproducible Deep Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21275)
*Kashish Mittal,Di Yu,Roozbeh Ketabi,Arushi Arora,Brendon Lapp,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对大规模深度学习训练中的数据加载瓶颈，本文通过优化架构（推送计算、本地缓存）和消除竞争条件，将训练速度提升6倍（22小时降至3小时），GPU利用率从10-15%提升至60%以上，并实现了严格的确定性数据加载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数十TB数据集上训练大规模深度学习模型时，面临硬件利用率低和训练可复现性差的关键挑战。系统性能分析显示，网络I/O和CPU端的数据转换（如PyArrow转NumPy）导致GPU利用率低至10-15%，严重制约了训练效率。

Method: 提出一种优化架构，包含两项核心改进：1）采用推送式工作节点级转换结合Fanout-Cache本地磁盘缓存，最小化跨训练周期的冗余I/O和CPU开销；2）通过实现专用的轮询分发器和结果队列，以及现代化的RNG处理，消除多工作进程共享队列中的竞争条件，实现严格确定性的数据加载。

Result: 优化方案实现了6倍加速，将端到端训练时间从22小时缩短至3小时；GPU利用率提升至60%以上；显著降低了运行间的差异，实现了稳健、高吞吐量和可复现的大规模模型训练。

Conclusion: 通过系统性优化分布式GPU训练流水线中的数据加载瓶颈，本文提出的架构成功解决了硬件利用率低和训练可复现性差的问题，为大规模深度学习模型训练提供了高效、可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Training massive-scale deep learning models on datasets spanning tens of terabytes presents critical challenges in hardware utilization and training reproducibility. In this paper, we identify and resolve profound data-loading bottlenecks within distributed GPU training pipelines using the Petastorm data loader and Apache Parquet datasets. Through systematic profiling, we demonstrate that network I/O and CPU-bound data transformations (e.g., PyArrow to NumPy) constrain GPU utilization to as low as 10-15%. To address this, we propose an optimized architecture that features push-down worker-level transformations coupled with local-disk caching via Fanout-Cache, minimizing redundant I/O and CPU overhead across training epochs. Furthermore, we eliminate race conditions in multi-worker shared queues by implementing dedicated round-robin ventilator and result queues, alongside modernized RNG handling, achieving strict deterministic data loading. Our optimizations yield a 6x speedup, reducing end-to-end training time from 22 hours to 3 hours, increasing GPU utilization to over 60%, and drastically reducing run-to-run variance, enabling robust, high-throughput, and reproducible large-scale model training.

</details>


### [75] [Research on the efficiency of data loading and storage in Data Lakehouse architectures for the formation of analytical data systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21449)
*Ivan Borodii,Halyna Osukhivska*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文对比评估了三种主流Data Lakehouse系统（Apache Hudi、Apache Iceberg和Delta Lake）在Apache Spark平台上的数据加载与存储效率，发现Delta Lake在加载速度上最优，Iceberg在存储空间节省和稳定性上最佳，而Hudi在本次评估场景中表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据湖仓架构的兴起，数据工程师和架构师在选择具体实现方案时缺乏基于性能和存储效率的实证对比研究。本研究旨在通过系统化评估，帮助确定最适合不同数据类型和数据量场景的湖仓架构，为实际项目选型提供科学依据。

Method: 研究基于Apache Spark分布式数据处理平台，设计了四个顺序ETL流程（读取、转换、加载），对三种湖仓系统进行对比测试。实验使用结构化（CSV）和半结构化（JSON）数据，文件大小最高达7GB，从数据加载时间和文件系统中表存储体积两个核心维度评估系统效率。

Result: 实验结果表明：Delta Lake在各类数据量场景下均展现出最优的数据加载速度，适合对加载性能要求高的系统；Apache Iceberg在磁盘空间利用和稳定性方面表现最佳，适合存储效率和稳定性优先的场景；Apache Hudi在本研究的数据加载和存储评估中效果较差，但在增量更新和流处理场景可能具有潜在优势。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统对比了三种主流Data Lakehouse架构的性能特征，为数据工程师和架构师提供了科学的选型指南。研究结论表明不存在普遍最优的方案，架构选择需根据具体业务需求权衡加载性能与存储效率，Delta Lake和Iceberg各有优势场景。

Abstract: The paper presents a study of the efficiency of loading and storing data in the three most common Data Lakehouse systems, including Apache Hudi, Apache Iceberg, and Delta Lake, using Apache Spark as a distributed data processing platform. The study analyzes the behavior of each system when processing structured (CSV) and semi-structured (JSON) data of different sizes, including loading files up to 7 GB in size. The purpose of the work is to determine the most optimal Data Lakehouse architecture based on the type and volume of data sources, data loading performance using Apache Spark, and disk size of data for forming analytical data systems. The research covers the development of four sequential ETL processes, which include reading, transforming, and loading data into tables in each of the Data Lakehouse systems. The efficiency of each Lakehouse was evaluated according to two key criteria: data loading time and the volume of tables formed in the file system. For the first time, a comparison of performance and data storage in Apache Iceberg, Apache Hudi, and Delta Lake Data Lakehouse systems was conducted to select the most relevant architecture for building analytical data systems. The practical value of the study consists in the fact that it assists data engineers and architects in choosing the most appropriate Lakehouse architecture, understanding the balance between loading performance and storage efficiency. Experimental results showed that Delta Lake is the most optimal architecture for systems where the priority is the speed of loading data of any volume, while Apache Iceberg is most appropriate for systems where stability and disk space savings are critical. Apache Hudi proved ineffective in data loading and storage evaluation tasks but could potentially be effective in incremental update and streaming processing scenarios.

</details>


### [76] [Systematizing Blockchain Research Themes and Design Patterns: Insights from the University Blockchain Research Initiative (UBRI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21517)
*Chien-Chih Chen,Yitian Wang,Emma Nasseri,Yebo Feng,Lauren Weymouth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以大学区块链研究计划（UBRI）网络为案例，研究了将区块链学术研究转化为可部署系统和监管框架的制度机制，揭示了学术研究如何影响生产架构、监管开发和生态系统韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着区块链和数字资产生态系统的快速扩张，将学术研究转化为可部署系统和监管框架面临巨大挑战。尽管在密码学、共识机制、数字资产和治理方面取得了显著进展，但在生态系统规模上维持研究到部署转化的制度机制在理论上仍显不足。

Method: 以大学区块链研究计划（UBRI）网络作为长期学术与行业合作的代表性案例，基于2022年至2025年的研究成果和会议，综合分析技术和制度领域中反复出现的设计张力，抽象出连接研究贡献与部署约束及政策适应的系统级主题。

Result: 识别并综合了技术和制度领域中反复出现的设计张力，包括可扩展性与安全性、去中心化与治理、隐私与合规性之间的权衡；抽象出了连接研究贡献与部署约束及政策适应的系统级主题，提供了一个结构化的视角来理解学术研究如何影响生产架构、监管开发和生态系统韧性。

Conclusion: 为理解新兴去中心化基础设施中学术研究如何影响生产架构、监管开发和生态系统韧性提供了结构化的分析视角，强调了系统级主题和设计张力的重要性，而非仅仅关注个别项目。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of blockchain and digital asset ecosystems has intensified the challenge of translating academic research into deployable systems and regulatory frameworks. While advances in cryptography, consensus, digital assets, and governance are substantial, institutional mechanisms that sustain research-to-deployment translation at ecosystem scale remain comparatively under-theorized. This paper examines the architectural and coordination patterns that enable such translation, using the University Blockchain Research Initiative (UBRI) network as a representative case of long-term academic and industry collaboration. Drawing on research outputs and convenings from 2022 to 2025, we synthesize recurring design tensions across technical and institutional domains, including scalability versus security, decentralization versus governance, and privacy versus compliance. Rather than cataloging individual projects, we abstract system-level themes that connect research contributions to deployment constraints and policy adaptation, providing a structured lens for understanding how academic research informs production architectures, regulatory development, and ecosystem resilience in emerging decentralized infrastructures.

</details>


### [77] [Leveraging SIMD for Accelerating Large-number Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21566)
*Subhrajit Das,Abhishek Bichhawat,Yuvraj Patel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出DigitsOnTurbo(DoT)，一种面向SIMD的大数运算新方法，相比传统算法实现加法减法最高1.85倍加速，乘法2.3倍加速；集成至现有库后实现端到端科学计算吞吐量提升19.3%，密码学应用延迟降低7.9%、吞吐量提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数算术在科学计算和密码学领域广泛应用，但传统算法固有的数据依赖性限制了SIMD并行化的应用，导致现代CPU的SIMD能力未被充分利用。

Method: 提出DigitsOnTurbo(DoT)，采用全新的计算重组策略：围绕独立的数据并行操作重构计算流程，而非对标准算法进行向量化，从而有效利用SIMD提供的并行计算优势。

Result: 相比现有SIMD实现：加法减法加速比达1.85倍，乘法达2.3倍；集成至最新库后加法减法加速比达4倍，乘法达2倍；端到端应用中，科学计算吞吐量提升最高19.3%，密码学实现延迟降低7.9%、吞吐量提升5.9%。

Conclusion: DoT通过算法重构有效克服了大数运算的数据依赖问题，显著释放了SIMD并行潜力，为科学计算和密码学应用带来实质性的性能提升，证明了数据并行计算范式在该领域的有效性。

Abstract: Large-number arithmetic, widely used in scientific computing and cryptography, has seen limited adoption of single instruction, multiple data (SIMD) parallelism on modern CPUs due to the inherent dependencies in traditional algorithms. We present DigitsOnTurbo (DoT), which restructures the computation around independent, data-parallel operations, rather than vectorizing the standard algorithms, thereby leveraging the benefits provided by SIMD. Over prior SIMD implementations, DoT achieves up to 1.85x speedups for addition and subtraction, and 2.3x for multiplication. When integrated into state-of-the-art libraries, DoT yields up to 4x speedup for addition and subtraction, and up to 2x speedup for multiplication, cascading into end-to-end throughput gains of up to 19.3% for scientific computations, and up to 7.9% latency and 5.9% throughput improvements on cryptographic implementations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [78] [Frequency-Forcing: From Scaling-as-Time to Soft Frequency Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20902)
*Weitao Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Frequency-Forcing方法，通过轻量级可学习小波包变换从数据本身提取低频辅助流，以软约束机制引导标准像素流实现频率有序生成，在ImageNet-256上持续改进FID，且可与语义流自然组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种频率有序生成范式各有局限：K-Flow采用硬频率约束需重写流坐标，Latent Forcing依赖重型预训练编码器。作者希望找到一种既能实现K-Flow的频率排序优势，又能保持Latent Forcing软约束路径保持特性的方法，同时避免外部依赖。

Method: 提出Frequency-Forcing方法：使用轻量级可学习小波包变换从数据本身提取低频"频率便签本"，构建自强迫信号作为辅助流；该辅助流比主像素流更早成熟，以软机制引导生成过程实现从粗到细的频率有序生成，同时保持核心流坐标不变。

Result: 在ImageNet-256数据集上，Frequency-Forcing相比强像素空间和潜空间基线持续改进FID指标；该方法可与语义流自然组合获得进一步增益；自强迫信号避免了外部依赖，其学习到的基比硬频率流中的固定基更适应数据统计特性。

Conclusion: 基于强迫机制的尺度排序是硬频率流的通用、路径保持替代方案；自强迫信号有效避免了外部依赖；所提方法证明了软约束频率引导在图像生成中的有效性和灵活性。

Abstract: While standard flow-matching models transport noise to data uniformly, incorporating an explicit generation order - specifically, establishing coarse, low-frequency structure before fine detail - has proven highly effective for synthesizing natural images. Two recent works offer distinct paradigms for this. K-Flow imposes a hard frequency constraint by reinterpreting a frequency scaling variable as flow time, running the trajectory inside a transformed amplitude space. Latent Forcing provides a soft ordering mechanism by coupling the pixel flow with an auxiliary semantic latent flow via asynchronous time schedules, leaving the pixel interpolation path itself untouched. Viewed from the angle of improving pixel generation, we observe that forcing - guiding generation with an earlier-maturing auxiliary stream - offers a highly compatible route to scale-ordered generation without rewriting the core flow coordinate. Building on this, we propose Frequency-Forcing, which realizes K-Flow's frequency ordering through Latent Forcing's soft mechanism: a standard pixel flow is guided by an auxiliary low-frequency stream that matures earlier in time. Unlike Latent Forcing, whose scratchpad relies on a heavy pretrained encoder (e.g., DINO), our frequency scratchpad is derived from the data itself via a lightweight learnable wavelet packet transform. We term this a self-forcing signal, which avoids external dependencies while learning a basis better adapted to data statistics than the fixed bases used in hard frequency flows. On ImageNet-256, Frequency-Forcing consistently improves FID over strong pixel- and latent-space baselines, and naturally composes with a semantic stream to yield further gains. This illustrates that forcing-based scale ordering is a versatile, path-preserving alternative to hard frequency flows.

</details>


### [79] [Reinforcing privacy reasoning in LLMs via normative simulacra from fiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20904)
*Matt Franchi,Madiha Zahrah Choksi,Harold Triedman,Helen Nissenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从小说中提取规范模拟物（normative simulacra）来微调LLM，使其学习上下文完整性（CI）隐私推理。通过监督学习结合GRPO强化学习，并设计复合奖励函数与对比评分机制，模型在法律合规基准测试和人类隐私期望相关性上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理的信息处理实践与用户的情境隐私期望普遍不匹配。现有方法要么通过主管-助手架构使推理成本翻倍，要么在狭窄的任务特定数据上进行微调，缺乏高效且通用的隐私对齐方案。

Method: 从虚构小说中提取规范模拟物（关于规范和信息流的结构化表示），采用监督微调（SFT）后接GRPO强化学习的两阶段训练。引入复合奖励函数（结合任务清晰度、结构完整性、内部一致性、上下文识别与LLM评判）和每次完成的对比评分机制，教导模型基于上下文进行推理而非记忆特定源规范。

Result: 在七个模型的评估中，SFT引入了限制信息流的保守先验，提高了对隐私相关情境的识别。结合规范基础（normative grounding）的GRPO在法律合规基准上得分最高，且与众包人类隐私期望的相关性最强。

Conclusion: 源自虚构作品的规范模拟物能够有效教授LLM情境隐私推理，使其成功迁移至现实世界领域，为LLM隐私对齐提供了一种新颖且有效的方法。

Abstract: Information handling practices of LLM agents are broadly misaligned with the contextual privacy expectations of their users. Contextual Integrity (CI) provides a principled framework, defining privacy as the appropriate flow of information within context-relative norms. However, existing approaches either double inference cost via supervisor-assistant architectures, or fine-tune on narrow task-specific data. We propose extracting normative simulacra (structured representations of norms and information flows) from fiction novels and using them to fine-tune LLMs via supervised learning followed by GRPO reinforcement learning. Our composite reward function combines programmatic signals, including task clarity (subsuming schema validity, construct discrimination, and extraction confidence), structural completeness, internal consistency, and context identification, with an LLM judge that evaluates whether the model's privacy reasoning is grounded in the held-out normative universe of the source text. To mitigate overfitting, we introduce per-completion contrastive scoring: each completion is evaluated against both the correct normative universe and a randomly selected wrong one, teaching the model to condition on context rather than memorize source-specific norms. We evaluate on five CI-aligned benchmarks spanning distinct societal contexts and ablate the contributions of RL and normative grounding. Across seven models, SFT introduces a conservative prior toward restricting information flow, improving recognition of privacy-relevant situations but not the correctness of privacy judgments. GRPO with normative grounding achieves the highest score on a law compliance benchmark and strongest correlation with crowdsourced human privacy expectations, demonstrating that fiction-derived normative simulacra can teach contextual privacy reasoning that transfers to real-world domains.

</details>


### [80] [Do Masked Autoencoders Improve Downhole Prediction? An Empirical Study on Real Well Drilling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20909)
*Aleksander Berezowski,Hassan Hassanzadeh,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次在井下钻探遥测数据预测中应用MAE预训练方法，最佳配置相比监督学习GRU基线降低19.8%的测试误差，但比LSTM基线高6.4%。发现潜空间宽度是主导架构选择，而掩码比例影响微乎其微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 井下钻探遥测存在根本性的标签不对称问题：地面传感器数据以1Hz连续生成，而有标签的井下测量数据成本高昂、间歇且稀缺。当前机器学习方法普遍采用全监督从头训练，不适应这种数据机制。

Method: 使用犹他FORGE地热井约350万个时间步的多变量钻探遥测数据，对72种MAE配置进行系统性的全因子设计空间搜索，在预测总泥浆体积任务上与监督学习LSTM和GRU基线进行对比。

Result: 最佳MAE配置相比监督GRU基线降低19.8%测试平均绝对误差，但比监督LSTM基线高6.4%。潜空间宽度是主导架构选择（与测试MAE的Pearson相关系数r=-0.59），而掩码比例影响可忽略，归因于1Hz钻探数据的高时间冗余性。

Conclusion: 建立了MAE预训练作为钻探分析可行范式的地位，并识别了其最有益的条件，为钻探遥测数据预测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Downhole drilling telemetry presents a fundamental labeling asymmetry: surface sensor data are generated continuously at 1~Hz, while labeled downhole measurements are costly, intermittent, and scarce. Current machine learning approaches for downhole metric prediction universally adopt fully supervised training from scratch, which is poorly suited to this data regime. We present the first empirical evaluation of masked autoencoder (MAE) pretraining for downhole drilling metric prediction. Using two publicly available Utah FORGE geothermal wells comprising approximately 3.5 million timesteps of multivariate drilling telemetry, we conduct a systematic full-factorial design space search across 72 MAE configurations and compare them against supervised LSTM and GRU baselines on the task of predicting Total Mud Volume. Results show that the best MAE configuration reduces test mean absolute error by 19.8\% relative to the supervised GRU baseline, while trailing the supervised LSTM baseline by 6.4\%. Analysis of design dimensions reveals that latent space width is the dominant architectural choice (Pearson $r = -0.59$ with test MAE), while masking ratio has negligible effect, an unexpected finding attributed to high temporal redundancy in 1~Hz drilling data. These results establish MAE pretraining as a viable paradigm for drilling analytics and identify the conditions under which it is most beneficial.

</details>


### [81] [FairyFuse: Multiplication-Free LLM Inference on CPUs via Fused Ternary Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20913)
*Fei Zuo,Xiaoyan Xi,Quanyi Zeng,Feiyu Wang,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairyFuse是一种面向CPU的LLM推理系统，通过三值权重融合消除浮点乘法，在单颗Intel Xeon上实现32.4 tokens/s，比llama.cpp Q4_K_M快1.24倍且精度近乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在仅CPU平台上的自回归生成受限于内存带宽。现有的权重量化方案虽减少内存压力，但仍需反量化并执行浮点乘法，限制了性能提升空间。

Method: 提出FairyFuse系统，将广泛线性层的8个实值子GEMV融合为单个AVX-512循环，利用掩码加法和减法实现无乘法执行，三值权重{-1, 0, +1}将乘法替换为条件加法、减法或空操作。

Result: Roofline分析显示16倍权重压缩将内存受限的GEMV转向计算受限；内核加速29.6倍。端到端在Intel Xeon 8558P上达到32.4 tokens/s，超越llama.cpp Q4_K_M 1.24倍；WikiText-2困惑度5.52（FP16为5.47），下游任务准确率66.0%。

Conclusion: 三值权重融合在带宽受限的CPU上有效消除乘法瓶颈，显著提升推理速度且保持近无损质量，而GPU平台收益有限，表明该方法特别适合CPU部署场景。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed on CPU-only platforms where memory bandwidth is the primary bottleneck for autoregressive generation. Weight quantization to four bits or below reduces memory pressure, yet existing systems still dequantize weights and perform floating-point multiplications, limiting the achievable gains. Ternary weights in {-1, 0, +1} provide a more efficient alternative, replacing multiplications with conditional additions, subtractions, or no-ops. While Fairy2i shows that ternary LLMs can match FP16 quality, its runtime does not exploit this structure. We present FairyFuse, an inference system that enables multiplication-free execution on commodity CPUs by fusing the eight real-valued sub-GEMVs of each widely-linear layer into a single AVX-512 loop using masked additions and subtractions, with zero floating-point multiplications. Roofline analysis shows that 16x weight compression shifts memory-bound GEMV toward the compute regime on bandwidth-limited CPUs, yielding a 29.6x kernel speedup while offering little benefit on GPUs. End-to-end, FairyFuse achieves 32.4 tokens per second on a single Intel Xeon 8558P, outperforming llama.cpp Q4_K_M by 1.24x with near-lossless quality (WikiText-2 perplexity 5.52 vs. 5.47 FP16; downstream accuracy 66.0%).

</details>


### [82] [Absorber LLM: Harnessing Causal Synchronization for Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20915)
*Zhixin Zhang,Shabo Zhang,Chengcan Wu,Zeming Wei,Meng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Absorber LLM，通过将长上下文吸收到模型参数中（自监督因果同步），在保持生成效果的同时显著降低推理内存开销，解决了Transformer计算成本高和现有参数记忆方法易过拟合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的自注意力机制随序列长度增加导致计算成本和内存消耗过高，限制了长序列推理；现有的RNN和SSM等固定内存替代方案会丢失长尾依赖关系；而将上下文记忆到参数中的方法（如TTT）容易过拟合于token级投影，且无法保留预训练LLM中上下文的因果效应。

Method: 提出Absorber LLM，将长上下文保留问题建模为自监督因果同步任务：在将历史上下文吸收到参数后，无上下文的模型应在未来生成上与拥有完整上下文的原始模型相匹配。通过同步更新模型的内部行为与原始模型来优化该目标，确保上下文吸收和泛化能力。

Result: 在长上下文和流式基准测试上的实验表明，Absorber LLM在降低推理内存的同时，相比先前的“参数即记忆”基线方法提高了准确性。

Conclusion: Absorber LLM成功实现了将长上下文信息吸收到模型参数中，在减少推理内存消耗和提升准确性方面优于现有基线方法，为长上下文处理提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformers suffer from a high computational cost that grows with sequence length for self-attention, making inference in long streams prohibited by memory consumption. Constant-memory alternatives such as RNNs and SSMs compress history into states with fixed size and thus lose long-tail dependencies, while methods that memorize contexts into parameters, such as Test-Time Training (TTT), are prone to overfitting token-level projection and fail to preserve the causal effect of context in pretrained LLMs. We propose Absorber LLM, which formulates long-context retention as a self-supervised causal synchronization: after absorbing historical contexts into parameters, a contextless model should match the original model with full context on future generations. We optimize this objective by synchronizing internal behaviors of the updated model with the original one, ensuring context absorption and generalization. Experiments on long-context and streaming benchmarks show that Absorber LLM reduces inference memory and improves accuracy over prior parameter-as-memory baselines.

</details>


### [83] [The Path Not Taken: Duality in Reasoning about Program Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20917)
*Eshgin Hasanov,Md Mahadi Hassan Sibat,Santu Karmaker,Aashish Yadavally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DexBench基准，通过正向预测程序行为和反向推断输入变异的双重任务，更准确地评估大语言模型对程序动态执行的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注预测特定输入下的程序属性（如代码覆盖率、程序输出），视角狭窄且易受数据污染影响，无法真正评估模型对程序执行流程的理解能力。

Method: 提出动态代码推理的双重性概念，包含两个互补任务：(1) 预测程序在给定输入下的观察行为；(2) 推断如何将输入变异以达到特定行为目标。基于此构建了包含445个配对实例的DexBench基准，并评估了13个LLM。

Result: 实验结果表明，双路径推理为动态代码理解提供了一个稳健且具有区分度的评估代理，能有效探测模型对执行流程的因果理解。

Conclusion: 通过正向和反向推理任务的结合，DexBench能够更全面、准确地评估大语言模型对程序动态执行的真正理解能力，为代码智能评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities across diverse coding tasks. However, their adoption requires a true understanding of program execution rather than relying on surface-level patterns. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on predicting program properties tied to specific inputs (e.g., code coverage, program outputs). As a result, they provide a narrow view of dynamic code reasoning and are prone to data contamination. We argue that understanding program execution requires evaluating its inherent duality through two complementary reasoning tasks: (i) predicting a program's observed behavior for a given input, and (ii) inferring how the input must be mutated toward a specific behavioral objective. Both tasks jointly probe a model's causal understanding of execution flow. We instantiate this duality in DexBench, a benchmark comprising 445 paired instances, and evaluate 13 LLMs. Our results demonstrate that dual-path reasoning provides a robust and discriminative proxy for dynamic code understanding.

</details>


### [84] [Forget, Then Recall: Learnable Compression and Selective Unfolding via Gist Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20920)
*Yuzhen Mao,Michael Y. Li,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSA（Gist Sparse Attention）方法，通过可学习的gist压缩token作为稀疏注意力的路由信号，实现从粗到细的上下文访问机制，在保持性能的同时显著降低长上下文处理的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型扩展到长上下文时面临全注意力机制二次方计算成本的挑战。现有的解决方案主要是KV-cache选择或压缩技术，但缺乏一种端到端可学习的方法来桥接这两种技术路径。

Method: 提出选择性展开机制：首先将上下文压缩为gist token，然后选择最相关的gist，最后恢复对应的原始块进行详细注意力计算。该方法使用交错gist压缩token作为稀疏注意力的路由信号，实现粗到细的机制。支持递归式的gist-of-gist层次化构建，实现多分辨率上下文访问和对数级解码复杂度。整个过程以端到端方式直接融入训练，无需外部检索模块或架构修改。

Result: 在LongBench和RAG基准测试上的实验表明，该方法在8×到32×的压缩比率下，持续优于其他压缩基线和推理时稀疏注意力方法。

Conclusion: GSA成功实现了压缩技术与稀疏注意力选择的有效桥接，通过端到端学习的粗到细机制，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持了模型性能，为长上下文处理提供了一种简洁有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling large language models to long contexts is challenging due to the quadratic computational cost of full attention. Mitigation approaches include KV-cache selection or compression techniques. We instead provide an effective and end-to-end learnable bridge between the two without requiring architecture modification. In particular, our key insight is that interleaved gist compression tokens -- which provide a learnable summary of sets of raw tokens -- can serve as routing signals for sparse attention. Building on this, we introduce selective unfolding via GSA, which first compresses the context into gist tokens, then selects the most relevant gists, and subsequently restores the corresponding raw chunks for detailed attention. This yields a simple coarse-to-fine mechanism that combines compact global representations with targeted access to fine-grained evidence. We further incorporate this process directly into training in an end-to-end fashion, avoiding the need for external retrieval modules. In addition, we extend the framework hierarchically via recursive gist-of-gist construction, enabling multi-resolution context access with logarithmic per-step decoding complexity. Empirical results on LongBench and RAG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms other compression baselines as well as inference-time sparse attention methods across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $32\times$. The code is available at: https://github.com/yuzhenmao/gist-sparse-attention/

</details>


### [85] [Validating a Deep Learning Algorithm to Identify Patients with Glaucoma using Systemic Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20921)
*John Xiang,Rohith Ravindranath,Sophia Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发并验证了一个仅使用系统性电子健康记录（EHR）数据的青光眼风险评估模型。该模型在独立机构数据上表现良好（AUROC 0.883），能通过常规医疗数据有效识别青光眼高危患者，有望实现无需专科影像检查的大规模预筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼的早期筛查通常依赖于专科影像检查，这在资源匮乏地区难以普及。研究旨在探索是否可以仅利用广泛可得的系统性电子健康记录（EHR）数据（如人口统计学、诊断、用药、化验结果等），开发一种可扩展、低成本且易于获取的青光眼风险评估模型。

Method: 研究采用横断面研究设计。作者将在All of Us国家数据上预训练的青光眼风险评估（GRA）模型，在斯坦福队列（20,636名患者，15%患有青光眼）上进行微调。模型输入仅包含人口统计学特征、系统性诊断、药物、实验室结果和体格检查测量值，不使用专科影像数据。通过AUROC、PPV等指标在留出测试集上评估模型性能，并分析了可训练层数和数据量的影响。

Result: 最佳模型在独立测试集上取得了0.883的AUROC和0.657的PPV。模型校准度良好，最高预测十分位数组的青光眼诊断率（65.7%）和治疗率（57.0%）最高。性能随着可训练层数的增加（最多至15层）以及更多数据的加入而提升。

Conclusion: 仅使用系统性EHR数据的青光眼风险评估模型具有可行性，能够有效识别高危患者。该方法无需依赖专业眼科影像设备，为实施可扩展且易于普及的青光眼临床预筛查提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We evaluated whether a glaucoma risk assessment (GRA) model trained on All of Us national data can identify patients at high probability of glaucoma using only systemic electronic health records (EHR) at an independent institution. In this cross-sectional study, 20,636 Stanford patients seen from November 2013 to January 2024 were included (15% with glaucoma). A pretrained GRA model was fine-tuned on the Stanford cohort and tested on a held-out set using demographics, systemic diagnoses, medications, laboratory results, and physical examination measurements as inputs. The best model achieved AUROC 0.883 and PPV 0.657. Calibration was consistent with clinical risk: the highest prediction decile showed the greatest glaucoma diagnosis rate (65.7%) and treatment rate (57.0%). Performance improved with more trainable layers up to 15 and with additional data. An EHR-only GRA model may enable scalable and accessible pre-screening without specialized imaging.

</details>


### [86] [ILDR: Geometric Early Detection of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20923)
*Shreel Golwala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ILDR（类间/类内距离比），一种基于几何的度量指标，能够在验证精度突增前检测到grokking现象，相比现有方法具有更早的检测时间和稳定性，并可用于节省训练时间和控制泛化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象描述了一种延迟泛化行为，即神经网络在验证准确率突增前早已达到完美的训练准确率。然而，现有的检测信号存在局限性：权重范数反映参数空间正则化且滞后于过渡期，而GrokFast的慢梯度EMA在无梯度放大时不稳定（标准差超过平均领先时间）。因此，迫切需要一种能够更早、更稳定地检测grokking过渡的直接信号。

Method: 作者提出了类间/类内距离比（ILDR），这是一种几何度量指标。它基于倒数第二层的表征计算，定义为类间质心分离与类内散度的比值。ILDR基于Fisher线性判别准则，无需特征分解，计算复杂度为O(|C|^2 + N)。重要的是，该指标仅在留出数据上评估，因此对记忆效应具有鲁棒性。当ILDR上升并超过基线值2.5倍时，即视为检测到grokking即将发生的早期信号。

Result: 在模运算和置换群组合（S5）任务上的实验表明，ILDR能够领先于验证准确率的grokking过渡9%到73%的训练预算，且领先时间随任务代数复杂度增加而增加。在8个随机种子下，ILDR平均领先950 ± 250步（变异系数26%）。grokking发生后，方差下降1696倍，印证了表征空间的急剧相变。利用ILDR作为早停触发器平均减少18.6%的训练时间；在ILDR阈值触发的优化器干预展示了双向控制能力。

Conclusion: ILDR作为一种早期检测信号，成功捕捉到了表征空间中预示泛化的几何重组，而非仅仅是下游的相关物。它为理解和控制grokking现象提供了有效工具，不仅显著提升了检测的时效性和稳定性，还能有效减少训练成本并实现对过渡的双向干预。

Abstract: Grokking describes a delayed generalization phenomenon in which a neural network achieves perfect training accuracy long before validation accuracy improves, followed by an abrupt transition to strong generalization. Existing detection signals are indirect: weight norm reflects parameter-space regularization and consistently lags the transition, while GrokFast's slow gradient EMA, used without gradient amplification, is unstable across seeds with standard deviation exceeding mean lead time. We propose the Inter/Intra-class Distance Ratio (ILDR), a geometric metric computed on second-to-last layer representations as the ratio of inter-class centroid separation to intra-class scatter. ILDR provides an early detection signal: it rises and crosses a threshold at 2.5 times its baseline before the grokking transition appears in validation accuracy, indicating early geometric reorganization in representation space. Grounded in Fisher's linear discriminant criterion, ILDR requires no eigendecomposition and runs in O(|C|^2 + N). It is evaluated exclusively on held-out data, making it robust to memorization effects. Across modular arithmetic and permutation group composition (S5), ILDR leads the grokking transition by 9 to 73 percent of the training budget, with lead time increasing with task algebraic complexity. Over eight random seeds, ILDR leads by 950 +/- 250 steps with a coefficient of variation of 26 percent, and post-grokking variance drops by 1696 times, consistent with a sharp phase transition in representation space. Using ILDR as an early stopping trigger reduces training by 18.6 percent on average. Optimizer interventions triggered at the ILDR threshold demonstrate bidirectional control over the transition, suggesting ILDR tracks representational conditions underlying generalization rather than a downstream correlate.

</details>


### [87] [Probably Approximately Consensus: On the Learning Theory of Finding Common Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21811)
*Carter Blair,Ben Armstrong,Shiri Alouf-Heffetz,Nimrod Talmon,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在在线审议平台中识别共识区间的新方法，通过降维将高维意见映射到一维空间，并利用ERM算法最小化查询成本，同时提供PAC学习保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线审议平台仅关注用户对具体陈述的偏好，忽略了议题的相对显著性和共识的更广泛延展性，因此需要一种能综合考虑议题分布与显著性的共识识别方法。

Method: 将潜在高维的意见数据通过嵌入与降维映射到一维意见空间，定义共识为一个区间，并设定一个最大化假设区间内期望一致性的目标函数；提出一种高效的实证风险最小化（ERM）算法，并建立了PAC学习保证。

Result: 初步实验展示了算法的性能，并验证了通过对现有陈述样本中的用户进行选择性查询，可以将所需查询数量减少到实际可行的规模。

Conclusion: 通过在低维意见空间建模共识区间并结合选择性查询策略，可以在保证理论学习性能的同时显著提升在线审议平台共识识别的效率。

Abstract: A primary goal of online deliberation platforms is to identify ideas that are broadly agreeable to a community of users through their expressed preferences. Yet, consensus elicitation should ideally extend beyond the specific statements provided by users and should incorporate the relative salience of particular topics. We address this issue by modelling consensus as an interval in a one-dimensional opinion space derived from potentially high-dimensional data via embedding and dimensionality reduction. We define an objective that maximizes expected agreement within a hypothesis interval where the expectation is over an underlying distribution of issues, implicitly taking into account their salience. We propose an efficient Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) algorithm and establish PAC-learning guarantees. Our initial experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithm and examine more efficient approaches to identifying optimal consensus regions. We find that through selectively querying users on an existing sample of statements, we can reduce the number of queries needed to a practical number.

</details>


### [88] [Clinically Interpretable Sepsis Early Warning via LLM-Guided Simulation of Temporal Physiological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20924)
*Weizhi Nie,Zhen Qu,Weijie Wang,Chunpei Li,Ke Lu,Bingyang Zhou,Hongzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM引导的时间模拟框架，通过显式建模疾病发作前的生理轨迹，实现了准确且可解释的脓毒症早期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症的及时且可解释的早期预警仍是重大临床挑战。传统的数据驱动模型虽然预测准确，但往往缺乏透明度（“黑盒”问题），限制了医生的信任度和临床适用性。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个核心组件的框架：1) 时空特征提取模块，捕获多变量生命体征的动态依赖关系；2) 医学提示前缀模块，将临床推理线索嵌入大语言模型；3) 基于智能体的后处理组件，将预测约束在生理合理的范围内。模型首先模拟关键生理指标的演变，再进行脓毒症发作分类。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上的评估显示，该方法在发病前24-4小时的预测任务中取得了优异的AUC得分（0.861-0.903），优于传统的深度学习和基于规则的方法。

Conclusion: 该模型不仅实现了优越的预测性能，还提供了与临床判断相符的可解释轨迹和风险趋势，能够辅助临床医生在重症监护环境中进行早期干预和个性化决策。

Abstract: Timely and interpretable early warning of sepsis remains a major clinical challenge due to the complex temporal dynamics of physiological deterioration. Traditional data-driven models often provide accurate yet opaque predictions, limiting physicians' confidence and clinical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided temporal simulation framework that explicitly models physiological trajectories prior to disease onset for clinically interpretable prediction. The framework consists of a spatiotemporal feature extraction module that captures dynamic dependencies among multivariate vital signs, a Medical Prompt-as-Prefix module that embeds clinical reasoning cues into LLMs, and an agent-based post-processing component that constrains predictions within physiologically plausible ranges. By first simulating the evolution of key physiological indicators and then classifying sepsis onset, our model offers transparent prediction mechanisms that align with clinical judgment. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV and eICU databases, the proposed method achieves superior AUC scores (0.861-0.903) across 24-4-hour pre-onset prediction tasks, outperforming conventional deep learning and rule-based approaches. More importantly, it provides interpretable trajectories and risk trends that can assist clinicians in early intervention and personalized decision-making in intensive care environments.

</details>


### [89] [Unsupervised Learning of Inter-Object Relationships via Group Homomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20925)
*Kyotaro Ushida,Takayuki Komatsu,Yoshiyuki Ohmura,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群操作层次关系的无监督表示学习方法，通过引入代数同态作为结构约束，实现了从动态图像序列中同时进行物体分割和运动定律提取，获得了物理可解释的解耦表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的深度学习模型依赖大数据集学习统计相关性，缺乏人类（特别是前语言期婴儿）那种从有限经验中自主获取世界底层结构并适应新情况的灵活性。研究旨在构建一个模拟婴儿认知发展的计算模型，探索如何从有限数据中学习物理世界的内在结构。

Method: 提出一种基于群操作层次关系的无监督表示学习方法，而非依赖统计独立性。该方法构建了集成架构，能够同时完成物体分割和从动态图像序列中提取运动定律。通过在神经网络中引入代数学中的同态概念作为结构约束，模型将像素级变化结构化分离为有意义的分解变换组件（如平移和变形）。

Result: 实验使用基于发展科学发现的交互场景（追逐和逃避任务），证明该模型可以在没有任何真实标签的情况下将多个物体分割到独立的槽位中。此外，物体之间的相对运动（如接近或远离）被准确映射并结构化到一维加性潜在空间中。实验表明，通过引入代数几何约束而非仅依赖统计相关学习，可以获得物理可解释的“解耦表示”。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，引入代数几何约束可以帮助系统获得物理可解释的解耦表示，这有助于理解婴儿如何将环境规律内化为结构的过程，为构建具有发展智能的人工系统提供了新视角。

Abstract: While current deep learning models achieve high performance by learning statistical correlations from vast datasets,which stands in stark contrast to human learning. They lack the flexibility of humans-particularly preverbal infants-to autonomously acquire the underlying structure of the world from limited experience and adapt to novel situations. In this study, we propose an unsupervised representation learning method based on a hierarchical relationship in group operations, rather than statistical independence, aiming to build a computational model of the cognitive development of infants. The proposed model features an integrated architecture that simultaneously performs object segmentation and the extraction of motion laws from dynamic image sequences. By introducing the Homomorphism from algebra as a structural constraint within a neural network, the model structurally separates pixel-level changes into meaningful, decomposed transformation components, such as translation and deformation. Using interaction scenes (chasing and evading tasks) based on developmental science findings, we experimentally demonstrate that the model can segment multiple objects into individual slots without any ground-truth labels. Furthermore, we confirmed that relative movements between objects, such as approaching or receding, are accurately mapped and structured into a one-dimensional additive latent space. These results suggest that by introducing algebraic geometric constraints rather than relying solely on statistical correlation learning, physically interpretable "disentangled representations" can be acquired. This study contributes to the understanding of the process by which infants internalize environmental laws as structures and provides a new perspective for constructing artificial systems with developmental intelligence.

</details>


### [90] [Domain-Aware Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for Semi-Supervised Generalization Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20928)
*Junyu Ren,Wensheng Gan,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了域感知分层对比学习框架(DAHCL)，通过域感知学习模块校正伪标签偏差，结合分层对比学习实现不确定样本的有效利用，解决了半监督域泛化故障诊断中伪标签偏差和样本利用不均衡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督域泛化故障诊断(SSDGFD)面临两个耦合的局限性：(1)伪标签生成主要依赖标记源域知识，忽略域特定几何差异，导致跨域伪标签系统偏差；(2)对未标记样本采用硬性接受或丢弃策略，刚性阈值导致跨域样本利用不均衡，不确定样本的硬标签分配易引入额外噪声。

Method: 提出域感知分层对比学习(DAHCL)统一框架：(1)域感知学习(DAL)模块显式捕获源域几何特征，校正跨异构源域的伪标签预测，缓解伪标签生成中的跨域偏差；(2)分层对比学习(HCL)模块结合动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督，使不确定样本无需依赖不可靠硬标签即可贡献于表示学习。同时在SSDGFD评估协议中引入工程噪声以更好反映实际工业场景。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验证明了DAHCL方法的有效性，联合提升了监督质量和未标记样本的利用率。

Conclusion: DAHCL框架成功解决了SSDGFD中伪标签偏差和样本利用不均衡的关键问题，通过域感知学习和分层对比学习的协同作用，在含工程噪声的实际工业场景下实现了更鲁棒的故障诊断性能。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unseen operating conditions remains highly challenging when labeled data are scarce. Semi-supervised domain generalization fault diagnosis (SSDGFD) provides a practical solution by jointly exploiting labeled and unlabeled source domains. However, existing methods still suffer from two coupled limitations. First, pseudo-labels for unlabeled domains are typically generated primarily from knowledge learned on the labeled source domain, which neglects domain-specific geometric discrepancies and thus induces systematic cross-domain pseudo-label bias. Second, unlabeled samples are commonly handled with a hard accept-or-discard strategy, where rigid thresholding causes imbalanced sample utilization across domains, while hard-label assignment for uncertain samples can easily introduce additional noise. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework termed domain-aware hierarchical contrastive learning (DAHCL) for SSDGFD. Specifically, DAHCL introduces a domain-aware learning (DAL) module to explicitly capture source-domain geometric characteristics and calibrate pseudo-label predictions across heterogeneous source domains, thereby mitigating cross-domain bias in pseudo-label generation. In addition, DAHCL develops a hierarchical contrastive learning (HCL) module that combines dynamic confidence stratification with fuzzy contrastive supervision, enabling uncertain samples to contribute to representation learning without relying on unreliable hard labels. In this way, DAHCL jointly improves the quality of supervision and the utilization of unlabeled samples. Furthermore, to better reflect practical industrial scenarios, we incorporate engineering noise into the SSDGFD evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that...

</details>


### [91] [IRIS: Interpolative Rényi Iterative Self-play for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20933)
*Wenjie Liao,Like Wu,Liangjie Zhao,Shihui Xu,Shigeru Fujimura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS提出了一种基于Rényi散度的自适应自博弈微调框架，通过调节阶参数α动态平衡不同训练阶段的学习动态，仅需26k标注样本即可超越在200k数据上训练的监督微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自博弈微调方法（如SPIN、SPACE、SPIF）依赖于固定的散度机制，但不同散度在不同的分布差距下表现各异，单一选择无法在所有训练阶段提供最佳学习动态。需要一种能够根据训练进度自适应调整的方法。

Method: 提出IRIS框架：基于Rényi散度的自博弈微调方法，包含两个独立的倾斜风险项，通过阶参数α控制指数重要性权重。该方法将多种现有自博弈目标统一为特定α值下的特例，并采用自适应阶调度策略，在训练早期使用更锐利的重要性权重，接近收敛时转向更平滑的精细化调整。

Result: 在Zephyr-7B和Qwen2.5-3B模型上的十个基准测试中，IRIS平均得分达44.57%，迭代过程中持续提升。仅需26k标注样本即可超越在完整200k数据集上训练的标准监督微调方法，证明了其数据效率优势。

Conclusion: IRIS通过可调节的Rényi目标实现了自博弈微调方法的统一理论视角，自适应阶调度能有效适应分布差距变化，在多个模型和基准测试上展现出优于现有方法的性能和数据效率，为大语言模型的微调提供了新的理论框架和实践方案。

Abstract: Self-play fine-tuning enables large language models to improve beyond supervised fine-tuning without additional human annotations by contrasting annotated responses with self-generated ones. Many existing methods rely on a fixed divergence regime. SPIN is closely related to a KL-based regime, SPACE to a Jensen-Shannon-style objective via noise contrastive estimation, and SPIF to $χ^2$-regularized self-play. Since these divergences exhibit different strengths depending on the distributional gap between model and target, no single choice appears to provide favorable learning dynamics across training stages. We propose IRIS (Interpolative Rényi Iterative Self-play), a Rényi-based self-play fine-tuning framework with a continuously adjustable objective. IRIS decomposes into two independent tilted risk terms over annotated and synthetic data, with exponential importance weights controlled by the order parameter $α$. We show that several self-play objectives can be interpreted as limiting or representative regimes at particular values of $α$, providing a unified theoretical perspective on these methods. An adaptive order schedule further adjusts $α$ to the distributional gap, shifting from sharper importance weighting early in training to smoother refinement near convergence. Theoretically, we establish the fixed-point property of IRIS and analyze how $α$ controls gradient concentration. Experiments on Zephyr-7B and Qwen2.5-3B across ten benchmarks show that IRIS improves upon baselines, reaching 44.57\% average score with gains across iterations. In our setting, IRIS with only 26$k$ annotated samples surpasses standard supervised fine-tuning trained on the full 200$k$ dataset.

</details>


### [92] [Promoting Simple Agents: Ensemble Methods for Event-Log Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21629)
*Benedikt Bollig,Matthias Függer,Thomas Nowak,Paul Zeinaty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明n-gram模型在预测精度上可与神经网络媲美，且资源消耗更低；提出的Promotion集成算法在降低计算成本的同时实现了优异的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在对比轻量级自动机模型（n-grams）与神经架构（LSTM、Transformer）在流事件日志下一活动预测任务中的表现，探索在保证预测精度的同时降低计算资源消耗的方法。

Method: 在合成模式及五个真实流程挖掘数据集上进行实验对比；评估不同上下文窗口设置下的n-gram性能；提出一种动态选择模型的集成方法——Promotion算法，以减少传统投票集成的开销。

Result: 配备合适上下文窗口的n-gram模型能达到与神经模型相当的准确率，且资源需求显著更低；相比窗口化神经架构表现出的不稳定性，n-gram提供了稳定一致的准确率；基于Promotion算法的集成方法在真实数据集上匹配或超越了非窗口化神经模型的准确率，同时计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 在流事件日志的下一活动预测任务中，轻量级的n-gram模型结合高效的Promotion集成算法，可以作为神经模型的有力替代方案，在精度与计算效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: We compare lightweight automata-based models (n-grams) with neural architectures (LSTM, Transformer) for next-activity prediction in streaming event logs. Experiments on synthetic patterns and five real-world process mining datasets show that n-grams with appropriate context windows achieve comparable accuracy to neural models while requiring substantially fewer resources. Unlike windowed neural architectures, which show unstable performance patterns, n-grams provide stable and consistent accuracy. While we demonstrate that classical ensemble methods like voting improve n-gram performance, they require running many agents in parallel during inference, increasing memory consumption and latency. We propose an ensemble method, the promotion algorithm, that dynamically selects between two active models during inference, reducing overhead compared to classical voting schemes. On real-world datasets, these ensembles match or exceed the accuracy of non-windowed neural models with lower computational cost.

</details>


### [93] [Data-Driven Open-Loop Simulation for Digital-Twin Operator Decision Support in Wastewater Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20935)
*Gary Simethy,Daniel Ortiz Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CCSS-RS模型，一种用于污水处理厂数字孪生决策支持的受控连续时间状态空间模型，在Avedøre全规模基准数据集上相比Neural CDE基线降低RMSE 40-46%，能够处理43%的数据缺失和1-20分钟的不规则采样，实现了12-36小时规划范围内的准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水处理厂需要数字孪生式的决策支持工具，这些工具需要能够模拟工厂在预定控制方案下的响应、容忍不规则和缺失的传感器数据、并在12-36小时的规划时间范围内保持信息性。然而，使用全规模工厂数据满足这些要求仍然是一个开放的工程-AI挑战。

Method: 提出CCSS-RS（受控连续时间状态空间）模型，该模型将历史状态推断与未来控制和外源推演分离。模型结合了类型化上下文编码、预定和预测驱动因素的增益加权强制、半群一致性推演，以及针对重尾和零膨胀WWTP传感器数据的Student-t加hurdle输出层。

Result: 在包含906,815个时间步、43%数据缺失率和1-20分钟不规则采样的公开Avedøre全规模基准上，CCSS-RS在H=1000时达到RMSE 0.696和CRPS 0.349（10,000个测试窗口）。相比Neural CDE基线降低RMSE 40-46%，相比简化内部变体降低31-35%。四个案例研究表明：氧设定点扰动使预测铵在300-1000范围内偏移-2.3至+1.4；平滑设定点方案在多标准筛选中排名第一；仅上下文传感器中断使监测变量RMSE最多增加10%；铵、硝酸盐和氧在整个推演过程中比持久性预测更准确。

Conclusion: CCSS-RS模型成功建立了一个实用的学习型模拟器，用于工业废水处理中的离线场景筛选，与机械模型形成互补。该模型能够有效处理实际污水处理环境中的数据质量挑战，为运营决策提供了可靠的预测支持。

Abstract: Wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) need digital-twin-style decision support tools that can simulate plant response under prescribed control plans, tolerate irregular and missing sensing, and remain informative over 12-36 h planning horizons. Meeting these requirements with full-scale plant data remains an open engineering-AI challenge. We present CCSS-RS, a controlled continuous-time state-space model that separates historical state inference from future control and exogenous rollout. The model combines typed context encoding, gain-weighted forcing of prescribed and forecast drivers, semigroup-consistent rollouts, and Student-t plus hurdle outputs for heavy-tailed and zero-inflated WWTP sensor data. On the public Avedøre full-scale benchmark, with 906,815 timesteps, 43% missingness, and 1-20 min irregular sampling, CCSS-RS achieves RMSE 0.696 and CRPS 0.349 at H=1000 across 10,000 test windows. This reduces RMSE by 40-46% relative to Neural CDE baselines and by 31-35% relative to simplified internal variants. Four case studies using a frozen checkpoint on test data demonstrate operational value: oxygen-setpoint perturbations shift predicted ammonium by -2.3 to +1.4 over horizons 300-1000; a smoothed setpoint plan ranks first in multi-criterion screening; context-only sensor outages raise monitored-variable RMSE by at most 10%; and ammonium, nitrate, and oxygen remain more accurate than persistence throughout the rollout. These results establish CCSS-RS as a practical learned simulator for offline scenario screening in industrial wastewater treatment, complementary to mechanistic models.

</details>


### [94] [Sink-Token-Aware Pruning for Fine-Grained Video Understanding in Efficient Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20937)
*Kibum Kim,Jiwan Kim,Kyle Min,Yueqi Wang,Jinyoung Moon,Julian McAuley,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有无训练视觉令牌剪枝方法在细粒度视频理解任务上性能急剧下降的问题，提出了一种即插即用的Sink-Token感知剪枝方法（SToP），通过抑制语义信息贫乏但吸引过多注意力的沉没令牌，在保持高达90%剪枝率的同时显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练视觉令牌剪枝方法主要在多项选择问答（MCQA）基准上验证，但在需要精确视觉定位的细粒度理解任务（如幻觉评估）上存在严重的性能崩塌。本文旨在探究这一性能差距的原因，发现沉没令牌（semantically uninformative tokens that attract excessive attention）是阻碍细粒度视频理解的关键障碍。

Method: 提出Sink-Token-aware Pruning（SToP）方法，该方法引入沉没分数（sink score）来量化每个令牌成为沉没令牌的倾向，并将该分数应用于现有的空间和时间剪枝方法中，主动抑制这些沉没令牌，从而增强视频理解能力。该方法是一种简单有效的即插即用方案。

Result: 将SToP应用于最先进的剪枝方法（VisionZip、FastVid和Holitom），在涵盖幻觉评估、开放性生成、组合推理和MCQA的多种基准测试上进行验证。结果显示SToP显著提升了性能，即使在剪枝高达90%视觉令牌的情况下依然有效。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统分析揭示了沉没令牌是影响细粒度视频理解的关键因素，提出的SToP方法通过感知并抑制沉没令牌，成功弥合了高效剪枝与精细理解之间的差距，为视频大模型的推理加速提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) incur high inference latency due to a large number of visual tokens provided to LLMs. To address this, training-free visual token pruning has emerged as a solution to reduce computational costs; however, existing methods are primarily validated on Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) benchmarks, where coarse-grained cues often suffice. In this work, we reveal that these methods suffer a sharp performance collapse on fine-grained understanding tasks requiring precise visual grounding, such as hallucination evaluation. To explore this gap, we conduct a systematic analysis and identify sink tokens--semantically uninformative tokens that attract excessive attention--as a key obstacle to fine-grained video understanding. When these sink tokens survive pruning, they distort the model's visual evidence and hinder fine-grained understanding. Motivated by these insights, we propose Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP), a simple yet effective plug-and-play method that introduces a sink score to quantify each token's tendency to behave as a sink and applies this score to existing spatial and temporal pruning methods to suppress them, thereby enhancing video understanding. To validate the effectiveness of SToP, we apply it to state-of-the-art pruning methods (VisionZip, FastVid, and Holitom) and evaluate it across diverse benchmarks covering hallucination, open-ended generation, compositional reasoning, and MCQA. Our results demonstrate that SToP significantly boosts performance, even when pruning up to 90% of visual tokens.

</details>


### [95] [HARBOR: Automated Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20938)
*Biswa Sengupta,Jinhua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长周期语言模型智能体，本文主张其“线束（harness）”设计是首要的机器学习问题，并提出HARBOR方法实现自动化的线束配置优化，证明其在超越少量比特的标志空间下优于人工调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于长周期语言模型智能体，其代码量和运行复杂度主要取决于包裹基础模型的“线束”（包括上下文压缩、工具缓存、语义记忆等组件），而非模型本身。一旦配置标志空间超过少量比特，人工堆叠配置的方式就变得低效，因此需要将线束设计视为一流的机器学习问题并实现自动化配置搜索。

Method: 提出HARBOR（Harness Axis-aligned Regularized Bayesian Optimization Routine）方法：1）形式化自动化线束优化为约束噪声贝叶斯优化问题，处理混合变量、成本异构的配置空间，并引入冷启动修正奖励和后验机会约束安全检查；2）构建块加性SAAS代理模型、多保真度成本感知采集函数和TuRBO信任区域；3）该方法适用于任何具有有界标志空间和可复现任务集的智能体线束。

Result: 在生产编码智能体上实例化标志门控线束问题，进行了受控的四轮人工调参案例研究（固定任务集）与端到端HARBOR运行对比，验证了自动化线束优化方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 线束设计是关键的机器学习问题，自动化配置搜索在超越少量比特的标志空间下优于人工堆叠；所提框架和方法具有任务类别无关性，可广泛应用于各类智能体线束的自动化优化。

Abstract: Long-horizon language-model agents are dominated, in lines of code and in operational complexity, not by their underlying model but by the harness that wraps it: context compaction, tool caching, semantic memory, trajectory reuse, speculative tool prediction, and the glue that binds the model to a sandboxed execution environment. We argue that harness design is a first-class machine-learning problem and that automated configuration search dominates manual stacking once the flag space exceeds a handful of bits. We defend this claim in two steps. First, we formalize automated harness optimization as constrained noisy Bayesian optimization over a mixed-variable, cost-heterogeneous configuration space with cold-start-corrected rewards and a posterior chance-constrained safety check, and give a reference solver, HARBOR (Harness Axis-aligned Regularized Bayesian Optimization Routine), built from a block-additive SAAS surrogate, multi-fidelity cost-aware acquisition, and TuRBO trust regions. Second, we instantiate the problem in a flag-gated harness over a production coding agent and report a controlled four-round manual-tuning case study against a fixed task suite and an end-to-end HARBOR run. The formulation itself is task-class agnostic: the configuration space, reward correction, acquisition, and safety check apply to any agent harness with a bounded flag space and a reproducible task suite.

</details>


### [96] [SCM: Sleep-Consolidated Memory with Algorithmic Forgetting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20943)
*Saish Sachin Shinde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCM（睡眠巩固记忆）架构，一种受神经科学启发的大语言模型记忆系统，通过引入工作记忆、重要性标记、睡眠阶段巩固、价值性遗忘和自我模型五大组件，解决了现有系统缺乏持久性、结构化和生物学合理性记忆的问题，实现了高召回率和低噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型记忆系统存在根本性局限：缺乏持久、结构化且具有生物学合理性的记忆机制。当前方法依赖于截断上下文窗口、无限增长的向量数据库或缺乏巩固与遗忘机制的分层存储系统，无法有效模拟人类记忆特性。

Method: SCM架构借鉴人类记忆机制，实现了五个核心组件：1）有限容量的工作记忆；2）多维重要性标记；3）包含NREM和REM不同阶段的离线睡眠期巩固机制；4）基于价值评估的意向性遗忘；5）支持内省的计算自我模型。

Result: 在包含八项测试的标准化基准测试中，原型系统在十轮对话中实现了完美的召回准确率，并通过适应性遗忘将记忆噪声降低了90.9%。即使存储了数百个概念，记忆搜索延迟仍保持在1毫秒以下。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了具备巩固、优先级排序和遗忘能力的记忆系统架构基础，为推进大语言模型记忆研究提供了一个可验证的实验平台。

Abstract: We present SCM (Sleep-Consolidated Memory), a research preview of a memory architecture for large language models that draws on neuroscientific principles to address a fundamental limitation in current systems: the absence of persistent, structured, and biologically plausible memory. Existing approaches rely on truncating context windows, growing vector databases without bound, or tiered storage systems that lack consolidation and forgetting mechanisms. SCM implements five core components inspired by human memory: a limited-capacity working memory, multi-dimensional importance tagging, offline sleep-stage consolidation with distinct NREM and REM phases, intentional value-based forgetting, and a computational self-model enabling introspection. Across a standardized benchmark suite of eight tests, the prototype achieves perfect recall accuracy over ten-turn conversations while reducing memory noise by 90.9% through adaptive forgetting. Memory search latency remains below one millisecond even with hundreds of stored concepts. This work establishes the architectural foundations for memory systems that consolidate, prioritize, and forget, offering a testable platform for advancing LLM memory research.

</details>


### [97] [LAF-Based Evaluation and UTTL-Based Learning Strategies with MIATTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20944)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对真实目标无法精确定义的机器学习应用场景，开发了EL-MIATTs框架的具体实现机制，包括基于LAF的评估算法和基于UTTL的学习策略，为不确定监督下的建模提供了理论基础和实践路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多现实世界的机器学习应用中，由于模糊性或主观性信息的存在，真实目标无法被精确定义。传统机器学习假设存在客观真实的"ground truth"，但在实际应用中这一假设往往不成立。EL-MIATTs框架基于"给定ML任务的真实目标在现实世界中客观不存在"的假设被提出，但缺乏连接理论与实践的具体实现机制。

Method: 研究提出了两个互补机制：(1) LAF(Logical Assessment Formula)评估算法：分析任务特定的MIATTs，研究其覆盖率和多样性如何决定结构特性并影响下游评估和学习；制定基于LAF的评估算法，可在原始MIATTs上操作或从中合成三元目标，平衡可解释性、合理性和完整性。(2) UTTL(Undefinable True Target Learning)学习策略：使用Dice和交叉熵损失函数，比较单目标和聚合优化方案；讨论LAF与UTTL如何连接逻辑语义与统计优化。

Result: 成功开发了完整的EL-MIATTs实施框架，包括：结构化的评估算法能够处理多个不准确目标；学习策略能够在不确定监督下进行有效的模型训练；LAF与UTTL的集成有效连接了逻辑语义和统计优化，弥合了理论与实践之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该研究为在"ground truth"本质上不确定的场景下开发ML系统提供了原则性基础，实现了EL-MIATTs框架从理论到实践的完整路径，为处理模糊性和主观性信息的机器学习应用提供了逻辑连贯且实际可行的建模方案。

Abstract: In many real-world machine learning (ML) applications, the true target cannot be precisely defined due to ambiguity or subjectivity information. To address this challenge, under the assumption that the true target for a given ML task is not assumed to exist objectively in the real world, the EL-MIATTs (Evaluation and Learning with Multiple Inaccurate True Targets) framework has been proposed. Bridging theory and practice in implementing EL-MIATTs, in this paper, we develop two complementary mechanisms: LAF (Logical Assessment Formula)-based evaluation algorithms and UTTL (Undefinable True Target Learning)-based learning strategies with MIATTs, which together enable logically coherent and practically feasible modeling under uncertain supervision. We first analyze task-specific MIATTs, examining how their coverage and diversity determine their structural property and influence downstream evaluation and learning. Based on this understanding, we formulate LAF-grounded evaluation algorithms that operate either on original MIATTs or on ternary targets synthesized from them, balancing interpretability, soundness, and completeness. For model training, we introduce UTTL-grounded learning strategies using Dice and cross-entropy loss functions, comparing per-target and aggregated optimization schemes. We also discuss how the integration of LAF and UTTL bridges the gap between logical semantics and statistical optimization. Together, these components provide a coherent pathway for implementing EL-MIATTs, offering a principled foundation for developing ML systems in scenarios where the notion of "ground truth" is inherently uncertain. An application of this work's results is presented as part of the study available at https://www.qeios.com/read/EZWLSN.

</details>


### [98] [Early Detection of Latent Microstructure Regimes in Limit Order Books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20949)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Vruksha Arun Hiremath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对限价订单簿从稳定到压力状态的突变，提出了一种三阶段因果模型来识别潜在恶化阶段，并设计了基于触发器的检测器，实现了显著的正向提前量，优于传统反应式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的早期预警信号（如订单流失衡和短期波动性）本质上是反应性的，无法在压力状态出现前进行有效预测。限价订单簿可能从稳定状态迅速转变为压力状态，需要一种能够识别潜在恶化阶段的方法，以便在可观察的压力出现之前提供预测窗口。

Method: 作者提出了一个三阶段因果数据生成过程（稳定→潜在累积→压力），在温和的时间漂移和状态持续性假设下，证明了潜在累积阶段的可识别性，并推导出严格正向的期望提前量和非平凡早期检测概率的保证。具体实现上，提出了一种基于触发器的检测器，结合了互补信号通道的MAX聚合、上升沿条件和自适应阈值技术。

Result: 在200次模拟实验中，该方法实现了平均+18.6±3.2个时间步的提前量，具有完美的精确度和适度的覆盖率，优于经典的变点检测和微观结构基线方法。在对一周BTC/USDT订单簿数据的初步应用中，该方法显示了一致的正向提前量，而基线方法仍然保持反应性。在低信噪比和短累积状态下，结果会退化，这与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了在订单簿压力状态出现前的潜在恶化阶段是可识别的，所提出的方法能够提供有保证的早期检测能力，为金融市场风险预警提供了新的理论框架和实践工具。然而，方法在低信噪比和短期累积场景下性能会受到限制，这为未来研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Limit order books can transition rapidly from stable to stressed conditions, yet standard early-warning signals such as order flow imbalance and short-term volatility are inherently reactive. We formalise this limitation via a three-regime causal data-generating process (stable $\to$ latent build-up $\to$ stress) in which a latent deterioration phase creates a prediction window prior to observable stress. Under mild assumptions on temporal drift and regime persistence, we establish identifiability of the latent build-up regime and derive guarantees for strictly positive expected lead-time and non-trivial probability of early detection. We propose a trigger-based detector combining MAX aggregation of complementary signal channels, a rising-edge condition, and adaptive thresholding. Across 200 simulations, the method achieves mean lead-time $+18.6 \pm 3.2$ timesteps with perfect precision and moderate coverage, outperforming classical change-point and microstructure baselines. A preliminary application to one week of BTC/USDT order book data shows consistent positive lead-times while baselines remain reactive. Results degrade in low signal-to-noise and short build-up regimes, consistent with theory.

</details>


### [99] [Differentially Private Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20985)
*Qichuan Yin,Manzil Zaheer,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种后处理技术（随机选择和线性组合），能够利用一组已有的不同隐私-效用权衡的模型，无需额外训练即可满足任意目标差分隐私要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习应用中，由于政策、法规或用户体验的变化，推理或部署阶段的隐私要求可能会不断改变。现有的方法难以在不重新训练模型的情况下灵活适应这些变化的隐私需求。

Method: 提出了两种后处理技术：随机选择和线性组合。这两种方法基于Rényi DP和隐私损失分布的视角进行隐私会计分析，能够从一组在相同数据集上训练但具有不同隐私/效用权衡的现有模型中，输出满足任意目标隐私参数的最终私有模型。

Result: 在私有均值估计的案例研究中，完整刻画了隐私/效用结果，并从理论上证明了线性组合方法优于随机选择方法。在多个模型以及合成和真实数据集上进行了实证验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的后处理方法能够灵活适应变化的隐私要求，无需额外训练成本，其中线性组合方法在理论和实践中都表现出更优的性能，为动态隐私需求场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In machine learning applications, privacy requirements during inference or deployment time could change constantly due to varying policies, regulations, or user experience. In this work, we aim to generate a magnitude of models to satisfy any target differential privacy (DP) requirement without additional training steps, given a set of existing models trained on the same dataset with different privacy/utility tradeoffs. We propose two post processing techniques, namely random selection and linear combination, to output a final private model for any target privacy parameter. We provide privacy accounting of these approaches from the lens of R'enyi DP and privacy loss distributions for general problems. In a case study on private mean estimation, we fully characterize the privacy/utility results and theoretically establish the superiority of linear combination over random selection. Empirically, we validate our approach and analyses on several models and both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [100] [Droplet-LNO: Physics-Informed Laplace Neural Operators for Accurate Prediction of Droplet Spreading Dynamics on Complex Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20993)
*Ganesh Sahadeo Meshram,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti,Suman Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息拉普拉斯算子神经网络(PI-LNO)，用于快速预测液滴在固体基底上的铺展过程，将计算时间从18-24小时大幅缩短，性能优于五种现有先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴在固体基底上的铺展是印刷、喷雾冷却和生物医学微流体系统的关键多物理场问题，但传统CFD仿真计算成本极高，单次瞬态计算需18-24小时，亟需高效的代理模型。

Method: 提出物理信息拉普拉斯算子神经网络(PI-LNO)，将拉普拉斯积分变换函数作为学习的物理信息泛函基，通过复拉普拉斯变换原生建模铺展过程的指数瞬态动力学；在TensorFlow框架下，基于接触角20°-160°的多表面CFD数据训练，采用结合Navier-Stokes方程、Cahn-Hilliard方程和因果性约束的物理正则化复合损失函数。

Result: 与UNet、UNet-AM、DeepONet、PI-UNet和LNO五种先进方法进行广泛对比基准研究，PI-LNO能够有效捕捉液滴铺展的指数瞬态动力学特性。

Conclusion: PI-LNO为液滴铺展等复杂多物理场瞬态问题提供了一种高效的计算替代方案，显著降低了计算成本并保持了物理一致性。

Abstract: Spreading of liquid droplets on solid substrates constitutes a classic multiphysics problem with widespread applications ranging from inkjet printing, spray cooling, to biomedical microfluidic systems. Yet, accurate computational fluid dynamic (CFD) simulations are prohibitively expensive, taking more than 18 to 24 hours for each transient computation. In this paper, Physics-Informed Laplace Operator Neural Network (PI-LNO) is introduced, representing a novel architecture where the Laplace integral transform function serves as a learned physics-informed functional basis. Extensive comparative benchmark studies were performed against five other state-of-the-art approaches: UNet, UNet with attention modules (UNet-AM), DeepONet, Physics-Informed UNet (PI-UNet), and Laplace Neural Operator (LNO). Through complex Laplace transforms, PI-LNO natively models the exponential transient dynamics of the spreading process. A TensorFlow-based PI-LNO is trained on multi-surface CFD data spanning contact angles $θ_s ε[20,160]$, employing a physics-regularized composite loss combining data fidelity (MSE, MAE, RMSE) with Navier-Stokes, Cahn-Hilliard, and causality constraints.

</details>


### [101] [SGD at the Edge of Stability: The Stochastic Sharpness Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21016)
*Fangshuo Liao,Afroditi Kolomvaki,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"自稳定"理论从全批量梯度下降(GD)扩展到小批量随机梯度下降(SGD)，推导出平衡态锐度间隙的闭式公式：ΔS = ηβσ²_u/(4α)，解释了为何SGD的锐度稳定在2/η以下，以及批量越小解越平坦的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全批量梯度下降(GD)训练神经网络时会出现"稳定边缘"(EoS)现象，即Hessian最大特征值(锐度)上升并悬停在2/η附近。已有研究用自稳定机制解释了这一现象。然而，对于小批量SGD，锐度会稳定在低于2/η的位置，且间隙随批量减小而增大，目前尚无理论解释这种锐度抑制现象。

Method: 作者提出"随机自稳定"框架，将自稳定理论扩展到SGD。核心洞察是：梯度噪声在顶部Hessian特征向量方向注入方差，增强了三次锐度降低力，将平衡点移至2/η以下。作者定义了相对于移动投影梯度下降轨迹的随机预测动力学，并证明了随机耦合定理，用于界定SGD与预测轨迹的偏差。

Result: 推导出平衡态锐度间隙的闭式公式：ΔS = ηβσ²_u/(4α)，其中α是渐进锐化率，β是自稳定强度，σ²_u是梯度噪声在顶部特征向量上的投影方差。该公式预测：(1)较小的批量会产生更平坦的解；(2)当批量等于完整数据集时，恢复为GD的情况。

Conclusion: 本文成功将自稳定框架扩展到SGD场景，从理论上解释了SGD中锐度抑制现象的内在机制。所提出的随机自稳定理论和推导的锐度间隙公式为理解批量大小与泛化性能的关系提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: When training neural networks with full-batch gradient descent (GD) and step size $η$, the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian -- the sharpness $S(\boldsymbolθ)$ -- rises to $2/η$ and hovers there, a phenomenon termed the Edge of Stability (EoS). \citet{damian2023selfstab} showed that this behavior is explained by a self-stabilization mechanism driven by third-order structure of the loss, and that GD implicitly follows projected gradient descent (PGD) on the constraint $ S(\boldsymbolθ)\leq 2/η$. For mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD), the sharpness stabilizes below $2/η$, with the gap widening as the batch size decreases; yet no theoretical explanation exists for this suppression.
  We introduce stochastic self-stabilization, extending the self-stabilization framework to SGD. Our key insight is that gradient noise injects variance into the oscillatory dynamics along the top Hessian eigenvector, strengthening the cubic sharpness-reducing force and shifting the equilibrium below $2/η$. Following the approach of \citet{damian2023selfstab}, we define stochastic predicted dynamics relative to a moving projected gradient descent trajectory and prove a stochastic coupling theorem that bounds the deviation of SGD from these predictions. We derive a closed-form equilibrium sharpness gap: $ΔS = ηβσ_{\boldsymbol{u}}^{2}/(4α)$, where $α$ is the progressive sharpening rate, $β$ is the self-stabilization strength, and $σ_{ \boldsymbol{u}}^{2}$ is the gradient noise variance projected onto the top eigenvector. This formula predicts that smaller batch sizes yield flatter solutions and recovers GD when the batch equals the full dataset.

</details>


### [102] [MCAP: Deployment-Time Layer Profiling for Memory-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21026)
*Anurita Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCAP，一种负载时逐层重要性估计方法，用于动态决策模型部署时的精度和显存放置，从而在异构硬件上实现更高效的推理。其系统NVE相比llama.cpp Q4_0实现了1.5-1.8倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大型语言模型部署到异构硬件时，主要瓶颈通常在于内存而非计算能力。现有方法难以在不同内存预算下灵活运行同一套权重，需要一种能够在负载时快速评估各层重要性并据此优化部署策略的方法。

Method: 提出MCAP（蒙特卡洛激活分析）方法，在加载时对每一层生成轻量级的重要性信号。基于该信号，系统动态决定每层的精度调度（如W4A8与W4A16之间选择）以及存储层级（GPU显存、RAM或SSD），使单一权重集能够适应多种内存预算环境。

Result: 构建的系统NVE在NVIDIA T4 GPU上，相比llama.cpp Q4_0实现了1.5到1.8倍的解码吞吐量提升，并使模型能够在之前不可行的内存受限环境中运行，且无需修改权重。

Conclusion: MCAP证明了负载时逐层重要性估计可以有效指导大型语言模型的动态精度分配和内存放置决策，显著提升了在内存受限异构硬件上的部署效率和灵活性。

Abstract: Deploying large language models to heterogeneous hardware is often constrained by memory, not compute. We introduce MCAP (Monte Carlo Activation Profiling), a load-time per-layer importance estimator that enables dynamic precision and memory placement decisions on the target device. MCAP produces a lightweight per-layer signal that drives both precision dispatch (W4A8 vs. W4A16) and residency tier (GPU, RAM, SSD), allowing a single set of weights to operate across diverse memory budgets. Our system, NVE, achieves 1.5-1.8x higher decode throughput than llama.cpp Q4_0 on NVIDIA T4 and enables models to run in memory regimes previously infeasible without modifying weights.

</details>


### [103] [A Deep U-Net Framework for Flood Hazard Mapping Using Hydraulic Simulations of the Wupper Catchment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21028)
*Christian Lammers,Fernando Arévalo,Leonie Märker-Neuhaus,Daniel Heinenberg,Christian Förster,Karl-Heinz Spies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于深度学习（U-Net）的替代模型，用于快速、准确地预测网格最大水位，可作为传统水力模拟的高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球洪水事件频发且破坏力增强，迫切需要开发快速可靠的洪水预测工具。传统方法依赖计算成本高昂的水力模拟，限制了预测效率。

Method: 通过对U-Net架构、图像块生成（patch generation）及数据处理进行一系列实验优化，构建了深度学习替代模型，以近似传统水力模型。

Result: 该框架在德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦地区Wupper流域的水力模拟数据上进行了测试，取得了与传统模拟相当的预测结果，同时显著提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的替代模型能够有效替代传统水力模拟，在保证预测精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为洪水预测提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing frequency and severity of global flood events highlights the need for the development of rapid and reliable flood prediction tools. This process traditionally relies on computationally expensive hydraulic simulations. This research presents a prediction tool by developing a deep-learning based surrogate model to accurately and efficiently predict the maximum water level across a grid. This was achieved by conducting a series of experiments to optimize a U-Net architecture, patch generation, and data handling for approximating a hydraulic model. This research demonstrates that a deep learning surrogate model can serve as a computationally efficient alternative to traditional hydraulic simulations. The framework was tested using hydraulic simulations of the Wupper catchment in the North-Rhein Westphalia region (Germany), obtaining comparable results.

</details>


### [104] [Synthetic Data in Education: Empirical Insights from Traditional Resampling and Deep Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21031)
*Tapiwa Amion Chinodakufa,Ashfaq Ali Shafin,Khandaker Mamun Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统对比了传统重采样与深度学习合成数据方法。结果显示存在基本权衡：重采样方法效用极高但隐私保护为零，深度学习模型隐私保护强但效用受损。变分自编码器（VAE）是最佳折中方案，在保证完全隐私保护的同时维持了83.3%的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成有望解决教育技术中的数据稀缺和隐私问题，但实践者在选择传统重采样技术还是现代深度学习方法时缺乏实证指导，目前缺乏系统性的基准比较研究。

Method: 使用10,000条学生成绩数据集，评估三种重采样方法（SMOTE、Bootstrap、随机过采样）和三种深度学习模型（自编码器、变分自编码器、Copula-GAN）。从分布保真度（KS距离、JS散度）、机器学习效用（TSTR分数）和隐私保护（最近记录距离）多个维度进行系统性基准对比。

Result: 发现重采样方法实现近乎完美的效用（TSTR: 0.997）但完全无法保护隐私（DCR ~ 0.00）；深度学习模型提供强隐私保证（DCR ~ 1.00）但效用显著降低。变分自编码器成为最佳折中选择，保持83.3%的预测性能同时确保完全隐私保护。

Conclusion: 提出了实践建议：在隐私可控的内部开发中使用传统重采样方法，在隐私至上的外部数据共享中使用VAE。本研究为学习分析中的合成数据生成建立了基础性基准和实用决策框架。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation offers promise for addressing data scarcity and privacy concerns in educational technology, yet practitioners lack empirical guidance for selecting between traditional resampling techniques and modern deep learning approaches. This study presents the first systematic benchmark comparing these paradigms using a 10,000-record student performance dataset. We evaluate three resampling methods (SMOTE, Bootstrap, Random Oversampling) against three deep learning models (Autoencoder, Variational Autoencoder, Copula-GAN) across multiple dimensions: distributional fidelity (Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance, Jensen-Shannon divergence), machine learning utility such as Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real scores (TSTR), and privacy preservation (Distance to Closest Record). Our findings reveal a fundamental trade-off: resampling methods achieve near-perfect utility (TSTR: 0.997) but completely fail privacy protection (DCR ~ 0.00), while deep learning models provide strong privacy guarantees (DCR ~ 1.00) at significant utility cost. Variational Autoencoders emerge as the optimal compromise, maintaining 83.3% predictive performance while ensuring complete privacy protection. We also provide actionable recommendations: use traditional resampling for internal development where privacy is controlled, and VAEs for external data sharing where privacy is paramount. This work establishes a foundational benchmark and practical decision framework for synthetic data generation in learning analytics.

</details>
